2026-10-24T05:09:53.0561794Z usr/libexec/anaconda/webui-desktop
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0562091Z #!/usr/bin/bash
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0562199Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0562424Z # This file is part of Cockpit.
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0562621Z #

2026-10-24T05:09:53.0567622Z # along with Cockpit; If not, see <http://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0567946Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0568190Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0568858Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0569515Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0570111Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0570547Z #
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0570752Z # Usage:
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0571121Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0571712Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0572244Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0572789Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0573248Z #
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0573457Z set -eu
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0573581Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0573686Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0573929Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0574173Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0574440Z do
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0574646Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0575746Z         in
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0575976Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0576276Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0576634Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0577031Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0577247Z     esac
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0577452Z done
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0577574Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0577750Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0578030Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0578148Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0578589Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0579043Z then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0579411Z     WEBUI_ADDRESS="0.0.0.0"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0579824Z fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0580085Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0580449Z echo "WEBUI_ADDRESS=$WEBUI_ADDRESS" > /tmp/webui-cockpit-ws.env
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0581044Z systemctl start webui-cockpit-ws

2026-10-24T05:09:53.0582137Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0582661Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0583214Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0583846Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0584341Z     #
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0585192Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0585850Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0586387Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0586968Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0587615Z     else
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0588150Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0589024Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0589628Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0589994Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0590599Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0591203Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0591642Z       fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0591999Z     fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0592266Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0592558Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0593164Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0593637Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0593964Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0594539Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0595309Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0595920Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0596374Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0596841Z     else
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0597284Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0597800Z     fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0598073Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0598384Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0598926Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0599366Z }
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0599652Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0599905Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0600628Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0601534Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0602163Z     timeout=60
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0602568Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0602857Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0603180Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0603722Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0604270Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0604669Z             exit 1
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0605294Z         fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0605710Z         sleep 1
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0605974Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0606260Z     done
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0606397Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0606717Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0607445Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0607974Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0608397Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0609013Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0609434Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0609620Z     else
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0609838Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0610107Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0610270Z     fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0610416Z }
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0610492Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0610566Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0610744Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0610854Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0611022Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0611368Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0611680Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0611887Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0611996Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0612241Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0612745Z     then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0613098Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0613583Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0613901Z     fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0614250Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0614591Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0614911Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0615562Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0616018Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0616285Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0616556Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" cp -a "${THEME_PATH}/." ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}

2026-10-24T05:09:53.0645246Z #!/bin/sh
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0645637Z # Functions to be used with livesys-* scripts
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0646150Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0646350Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0646746Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0647268Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0647794Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0648167Z fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0648371Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0648653Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0649151Z strstr() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0649461Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0649823Z }
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0650028Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0650225Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0650615Z action() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0650924Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0651162Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0651331Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0651672Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0652081Z     shift
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0652383Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0652726Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0653126Z     else
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0653447Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0653843Z     fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0654143Z     rc=$?
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0654466Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0654988Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0655307Z }
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0655512Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0655793Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0656313Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0656856Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0657373Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0657748Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0658171Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0658534Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0658982Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0659514Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0660064Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0660584Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0661129Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0661639Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0662618Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0665634Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0666120Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0666434Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0666649Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0667159Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0667720Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0668075Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0668258Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0668620Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0668949Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0669324Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0672425Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0672785Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0673163Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0673523Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0673879Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0674230Z         ;;
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0674524Z esac
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0674862Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0675062Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0675436Z success() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0675915Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0676351Z     return 0
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0676656Z }
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0676854Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0677104Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0677505Z failure() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0677810Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0678156Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0678621Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0679057Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0679530Z }
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0679726Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0679908Z echo_success() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0680253Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0680658Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0681013Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0681456Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0681875Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0682291Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0682611Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0682925Z     return 0
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0683227Z }
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0683413Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0683587Z echo_failure() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0683912Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0684300Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0684634Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_FAILURE
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0685243Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "FAILED")"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0685658Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0686076Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0686387Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0686691Z     return 1
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0686988Z }
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0687174Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0687346Z fscheck() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0687647Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0687976Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0688289Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0688637Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0689031Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0689364Z         ext[432])
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0690410Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0690794Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0691208Z             ;;
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0691530Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0691860Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0692227Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0692627Z             ;;
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0692930Z         btrfs)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0693306Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0693816Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0694266Z             ;;
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0694573Z         xfs)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0695166Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0695599Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0696002Z             ;;
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0696303Z         f2fs)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0696631Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0697050Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0697449Z             ;;
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0697749Z         *)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0698150Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0698673Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0699064Z     esac
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0699355Z }

2026-10-24T05:09:53.0744842Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0745282Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0745694Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0746160Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0746644Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0747047Z   fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0747284Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0747543Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0748054Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0748640Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0749184Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0749595Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0750055Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0750720Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0751281Z     fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0751645Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0752046Z   fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0752445Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0752822Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0753064Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0753494Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0753920Z     echo
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0754250Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0755161Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0755695Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0756099Z   fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0756514Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0757234Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0757745Z   fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0758002Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0758198Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0758639Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0759235Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0759870Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0760489Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0761009Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0761581Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0761913Z }
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0762087Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0762268Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0762558Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0762866Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0763293Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0763674Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0763969Z fi
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0764144Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0764348Z # if we have a persistent /home, then we want to go ahead and mount it
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0764887Z { ! strstr "$cmdline" nopersistenthome && [ "$homedev" ]; } &&
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0765549Z   action "Mounting persistent /home" mountPersistentHome
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0765942Z
2026-10-24T05:09:53.0766094Z [ "$configdone" ] && exit 0
